os\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HDF5_USE_FILE_LOCKING\"] = \"FALSE\"\n\nimport gc\nimport json\nimport time\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nnp.random.seed(42)\ntorch.manual_seed(42)\n\n# ── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b\"\nUNNORM_KEY = \"bridge_orig\"\nTASK_IDS = [0, 3, 7]\nN_EPISODES = 10\nMAX_STEPS = 300\nCONDITIONS = [\"baseline\", \"ablated\"]\nRESULTS_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/results\"\n\nNUM_IMAGE_TOKENS = 256\nINST_START = 1 + NUM_IMAGE_TOKENS  # BOS(1) + image(256) = 257\nINSTRUCTION_HEADS_24 = [0, 1, 2, 4, 7, 8, 10, 11, 12, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20,\n                        22, 23, 25, 26, 27, 29, 30, 31]\n\n# ── LIBERO env setup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nimport robosuite as suite\nsuite.load_controller_config = suite.load_part_controller_config\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\nimport libero.libero\n\nLIB_DIR = os.path.dirname(libero.libero.__file__)\n\n\ndef make_env(task_id):\n    bench = benchmark.get_benchmark(\"libero_goal\")()\n    task = bench.get_task(task_id)\n    bddl_path = os.path.join(LIB_DIR, \"bddl_files\", task.problem_folder, task.bddl_file)\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(\n        bddl_file_name=bddl_path,\n        camera_heights=224,\n        camera_widths=224,\n        camera_names=\"agentview\",\n    )\n    return env, task.language, task.name\n\n\n# ── Ablation hook ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndef install_ablation_hook(model, heads_to_ablate):\n    import transformers.models.llama.modeling_llama as llama_mod\n    from transformers.models.llama.modeling_llama import repeat_kv\n\n    layer1_attn = model.language_model.model.layers[1].self_attn\n    layer1_attn._ablate_heads = heads_to_ablate\n    original_fn = llama_mod.eager_attention_forward\n\n    def ablated_eager_attention_forward(module, query, key, value, attention_mask,\n                                        scaling, dropout=0.0, **kwargs):\n        key_states = repeat_kv(key, module.num_key_value_groups)\n        value_states = repeat_kv(value, module.num_key_value_groups)\n        attn_weights = torch.matmul(query, key_states.transpose(2, 3)) * scaling\n        if attention_mask is not None:\n            causal_mask = attention_mask[:, :, :, :key_states.shape[-2]]\n            attn_weights = attn_weights + causal_mask\n        attn_weights = nn.functional.softmax(attn_weights, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(query.dtype)\n        attn_weights = nn.functional.dropout(attn_weights, p=dropout, training=module.training)\n        if hasattr(module, '_ablate_heads') and module._ablate_heads is not None:\n            seq_len = attn_weights.shape[-1]\n            if INST_START < seq_len:\n                attn_weights[:, module._ablate_heads, :, INST_START:] = 0.0\n                row_sums = attn_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-9)\n                attn_weights = attn_weights / row_sums\n        attn_output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, value_states)\n        attn_output = attn_output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()\n        return attn_output, attn_weights\n\n    llama_mod.eager_attention_forward = ablated_eager_attention_forward\n    return original_fn\n\n\ndef remove_ablation_hook(model, original_fn):\n    import transformers.models.llama.modeling_llama as llama_mod\n    llama_mod.eager_attention_forward = original_fn\n    model.language_model.model.layers[1].self_attn._ablate_heads = None\n\n\n# ── Model loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndef load_model():\n    from transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\n    model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n        MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, trust_remote_code=True\n    ).to(\"cuda:0\")\n    model.eval()\n    return model, processor\n\n\ndef get_action(model, processor, image_array, instruction):\n    image = Image.fromarray(image_array)\n    prompt = f\"In: What action should the robot take to {instruction}?\\nOut:\"\n    inputs = processor(prompt, image).to(\"cuda:0\", dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    action = model.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key=UNNORM_KEY, do_sample=False)\n    return action\n\n\n# ── Main ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndef main():\n    os.makedirs(RESULTS_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\n    print(\"Loading model...\")\n    model, processor = load_model()\n    print(\"Model loaded.\")\n\n    import transformers.models.llama.modeling_llama as llama_mod\n    original_eager_fn = llama_mod.eager_attention_forward\n\n    bench = benchmark.get_benchmark(\"libero_goal\")()\n    all_results = {}\n    task_names = []\n\n    for task_id in TASK_IDS:\n        task = bench.get_task(task_id)\n        task_name = task.name\n        task_lang = task.language\n        task_names.append(task_name)\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Task {task_id}: {task_name} | {task_lang}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n        all_results[task_name] = {}\n\n        for condition in CONDITIONS:\n            print(f\"\\n  Condition: {condition}\")\n\n            if condition == \"ablated\":\n                install_ablation_hook(model, INSTRUCTION_HEADS_24)\n            else:\n                model.language_model.model.layers[1].self_attn._ablate_heads = None\n                llama_mod.eager_attention_forward = original_eager_fn\n\n            episodes = []\n            for ep in range(N_EPISODES):\n                env, _, _ = make_env(task_id)\n                np.random.seed(42 + ep)\n                obs = env.reset()\n\n                cumulative_reward = 0.0\n                success = False\n\n                for step_i in range(MAX_STEPS):\n                    image = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\n                    action = get_action(model, processor, image, task_lang)\n                    obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\n                    cumulative_reward += reward\n                    if env.env._check_success():\n                        success = True\n                        break\n\n                ep_result = {\n                    \"success\": bool(success),\n                    \"reward\": float(cumulative_reward),\n                    \"steps\": step_i + 1,\n                }\n                episodes.append(ep_result)\n                env.close()\n\n                status = \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"fail\"\n                print(f\"    ep {ep:2d}: {status} | reward={cumulative_reward:.3f} | steps={step_i+1}\")\n\n                if (ep + 1) % 10 == 0:\n                    sr = sum(e[\"success\"] for e in episodes) / len(episodes)\n                    print(f\"    --- {len(episodes)} eps done, SR={sr:.2f}\")\n\n            all_results[task_name][condition] = episodes\n\n            if condition == \"ablated\":\n                remove_ablation_hook(model, original_eager_fn)\n\n            gc.collect()\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    # ── Summary ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    baseline_successes = 0\n    ablated_successes = 0\n    total = 0\n    per_task = {}\n\n    for tname in task_names:\n        b_sr = sum(e[\"success\"] for e in all_results[tname][\"baseline\"]) / N_EPISODES\n        a_sr = sum(e[\"success\"] for e in all_results[tname][\"ablated\"]) / N_EPISODES\n        per_task[tname] = {\"baseline_sr\": b_sr, \"ablated_sr\": a_sr, \"delta\": b_sr - a_sr}\n        baseline_successes += sum(e[\"success\"] for e in all_results[tname][\"baseline\"])\n        ablated_successes += sum(e[\"success\"] for e in all_results[tname][\"ablated\"])\n        total += N_EPISODES\n\n    summary = {\n        \"baseline_success_rate\": baseline_successes / total,\n        \"ablated_success_rate\": ablated_successes / total,\n        \"delta\": (baseline_successes - ablated_successes) / total,\n        \"per_task\": per_task,\n    }\n\n    output = {\n        \"config\": {\n            \"n_tasks\": len(TASK_IDS),\n            \"n_episodes\": N_EPISODES,\n            \"conditions\": CONDITIONS,\n            \"max_steps\": MAX_STEPS,\n            \"task_ids\": TASK_IDS,\n            \"unnorm_key\": UNNORM_KEY,\n            \"model_path\": MODEL_PATH,\n            \"ablated_heads\": INSTRUCTION_HEADS_24,\n        },\n        \"tasks\": task_names,\n        \"results\": all_results,\n        \"summary\": summary,\n    }\n\n    out_path = os.path.join(RESULTS_DIR, \"behavioral_pilot.json\")\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPTEOF\necho \"Script written\"",

}

### Assistant

_source line 324_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The MAPPING dict is empty! `MAPPING: dict[str, str] = {}` and `NAMESPACES: dict[str, list[str]] = {}`. This means the editable install finder has no mappings registered, so it can't find the `libero` package.

This is because the `setup.py` install method (`python setup.py develop`) doesn't properly populate the modern editable finder. The package structure has nested `libero/libero/` which confuses the install.

Let me fix this by directly adding the LIBERO path to PYTHONPATH, or by manually adding the mapping.


The simplest approach is to just add `/root/autodl-tmp/LIBERO` directly to `sys.path` in the script so Python can locate the package.
```

</details>

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def eager_attention_forward(\\n    module: nn.Module,\\n    query: torch.Tensor,\\n    key: torch.Tensor,\\n    value: torch.Tensor,\\n    attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor],\\n    scaling: float,\\n    dropout: float = 0.0,\\n    **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],\\n):\\n    key_states = repeat_kv(key, module.num_key_value_groups)\\n    value_states = repeat_kv(value, module.num_key_value_groups)\\n\\n    attn_weights = torch.matmul(query, key_states.transpose(2, 3)) * scaling\\n    if attention_mask is not None:\\n        causal_mask = attention_mask[:, :, :, : key_states.shape[-2]]\\n        attn_weights = attn_weights + causal_mask\\n\\n    attn_weights = nn.functional.softmax(attn_weights, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(query.dtype)\\n    attn_weights = nn.functional.dropout(attn_weights, p=dropout, training=module.training)\\n    attn_output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, value_states)\\n    attn_output = attn_output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()\\n\\n    return attn_output, attn_weights\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 6.32}}"}

1. `Forward globals module: transformers.utils.deprecation` - The forward method's `__globals__` is from the `deprecation` module, not `modeling_llama`!
2. `eager_attention_forward in forward globals: None` - The `eager_attention_forward` function is NOT in the forward method's global scope!

This is because the `@deprecate_kwarg` decorator wraps the forward method, and the wrapper function's globals are from `transformers.utils.deprecation`, not from `modeling_llama`.

So when the forward method executes `attention_interface: Callable = eager_attention_forward`, Python looks for `eager_attention_forward` in the wrapper's globals (the `deprecation` module), where it doesn't exist. 

I'd expect this to raise a `NameError`, but the code runs fine, so I'm wondering if `functools.wraps` somehow preserves the original function's globals. Actually, no—`functools.wraps` only copies metadata like `__name__` and `__doc__`, not `__globals__`. The wrapper's globals come from wherever it was defined. So if the wrapper can't find `eager_attention_forward` in the deprecation module's namespace, how is this even working?

Oh—maybe the `__wrapped__` attribute is the key here. The decorator might be preserving a reference to the original function, and when the code accesses `__wrapped__`, it gets back to the original function with its original globals intact.

patched_forward(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask=None, past_key_values=None, cache_position=None, **kwargs):\n    input_shape = hidden_states.shape[:-1]\n    hidden_shape = (*input_shape, -1, layer1_attn.head_dim)\n\n    query_states = layer1_attn.q_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)\n    key_states = layer1_attn.k_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)\n    value_states = layer1_attn.v_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)\n\n    cos, sin = position_embeddings\n    query_states, key_states = apply_rotary_pos_emb(query_states, key_states, cos, sin)\n\n    if past_key_values is not None:\n        cache_kwargs = {'sin': sin, 'cos': cos, 'cache_position': cache_position}\n        key_states, value_states = past_key_values.update(key_states, value_states, layer1_attn.layer_idx, cache_kwargs)\n\n    key_states = repeat_kv(key_states, layer1_attn.num_key_value_groups)\n    value_states = repeat_kv(value_states, layer1_attn.num_key_value_groups)\n\n    attn_weights = torch.matmul(query_states, key_states.transpose(2, 3)) * keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-9)\n            attn_weights = attn_weights / row_sums\n\n    attn_output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, value_states)\n    attn_output = attn_output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()\n    attn_output = attn_output.reshape(*input_shape, -1).contiguous()\n    attn_output = layer1_attn.o_proj(attn_output)\n    return attn_output, attn_weights\n\nlayer1_attn.forward = {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def forward(\\n        self,\\n        input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\\n        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\\n        pixel_values: Optional[torch.FloatTensor] = None,\\n        labels: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\\n        inputs_embeds: Optional[torch.FloatTensor] = None,\\n        past_key_values: Optional[List[torch.FloatTensor]] = None,\\n        use_cache: Optional[bool] = None,\\n        output_attentions: Optional[bool] = None,\\n        output_hidden_states: Optional[bool] = None,\\n        output_projector_features: Optional[bool] = None,\\n        return_dict: Optional[bool] = None,\\n    ) -> Union[Tuple, PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Run a forward pass through the VLM, returning a PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast instance.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        output_attentions = output_attentions if output_attentions is not None else self.config.output_attentions\\n        output_hidden_states = (\\n            output_hidden_states if output_hidden_states is not None else self.config.output_hidden_states\\n        )\\n        output_projector_features = output_projector_features if output_projector_features is not None else False\\n        return_dict = return_dict if return_dict is not None else self.config.use_return_dict\\n\\n        # Respect `use_cache` only if not training (even if `gradient_checkpointing` is off)\\n        use_cache = use_cache and not self.training\\n\\n        # Instantiate Placeholder for Projector Features\\n        projected_patch_embeddings = None\\n\\n        # Note :: We only support forward passes with the following cases:\\n        #   => Cached Generation :: (input_ids.shape[1] == 1) and (past_key_values is not None)\\n        #   => Unimodal Forward :: (pixel_values is None)\\n        #   => Multimodal Forward :: (pixel_values is not None) and (input_ids/embeds.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0])\\n\\n        # === Handle Generation with Cache (`input_ids.shape[1] == 1`) =>> requires `past_keys_values` ===\\n        if input_ids.shape[1] == 1:\\n            assert input_ids.shape[0] == 1, \\\"Generation is only currently supported for batch size of 1!\\\"\\n            assert past_key_values is not None, \\\"You must provide `past_key_values` during cached generation!\\\"\\n            assert labels is None, \\\"Unexpected key `labels` provided during cached generation!\\\"\\n\\n            language_model_output = self.language_model(\\n                input_ids=input_ids,\\n                attention_mask=None,\\n                position_ids=None,\\n                past_key_values=past_key_values,\\n                inputs_embeds=None,\\n                labels=None,\\n                use_cache=use_cache,\\n                output_attentions=output_attentions,\\n                output_hidden_states=output_hidden_states,\\n                return_dict=return_dict,\\n            )\\n\\n        # === Handle Unimodal Forward ===\\n        elif pixel_values is None:\\n            assert (input_ids is not None) and (inputs_embeds is None), \\\"Missing `input_ids` in language-only forward!\\\"\\n            assert past_key_values is None, \\\"Unexpected key `past_key_values` provided during language-only forward!\\\"\\n\\n            language_model_output = self.language_model(\\n                input_ids=input_ids,\\n                attention_mask=attention_mask,\\n                position_ids=None,\\n                past_key_values=None,\\n                inputs_embeds=None,\\n                labels=labels,\\n                use_cache=use_cache,\\n                output_attentions=output_attentions,\\n                output_hidden_states=output_hidden_states,\\n                return_dict=return_dict,\\n            )\\n\\n        # === Handle Multimodal Forward ===\\n        elif (input_ids.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0]) or (inputs_embeds.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0]):\\n            assert past_key_values is None, \\\"Unexpected key `past_key_values` provided during language-only forward!\\\"\\n\\n            # Visual Feature Extraction\\n            patch_features = self.vision_backbone(pixel_values)\\n\\n            # Projection Logic =>> Update Attention Mask\\n            projected_patch_embeddings = self.projector(patch_features)\\n            projected_patch_attention_mask = None\\n            if attention_mask is not None:\\n                projected_patch_attention_mask = torch.full(\\n                    (projected_patch_embeddings.shape[0], projected_patch_embeddings.shape[1]),\\n                    fill_value=True,\\n                    dtype=attention_mask.dtype,\\n                    device=attention_mask.device,\\n                )\\n\\n            # Get Input Embeddings (from Language Model Embeddings)\\n            input_embeddings = self.get_input_embeddings()(input_ids)\\n\\n            # Build Multimodal Embeddings & Attention Mask =>> Prismatic defaults to inserting after &lt;BOS> token (1:)\\n            multimodal_embeddings = torch.cat(\\n                [input_embeddings[:, :1, :], projected_patch_embeddings, input_embeddings[:, 1:, :]], dim=1\\n            )\\n            multimodal_attention_mask = None\\n            if attention_mask is not None:\\n                multimodal_attention_mask = torch.cat(\\n                    [attention_mask[:, :1], projected_patch_attention_mask, attention_mask[:, 1:]], dim=1\\n                )\\n\\n            # Build Labels (if specified) =>> Ignore Labels for Patch Embeddings\\n            multimodal_labels = None\\n            if labels is not None:\\n                projected_patch_labels = torch.full(\\n                    (projected_patch_embeddings.shape[0], projected_patch_embeddings.shape[1]),\\n                    fill_value=IGNORE_INDEX,\\n                    dtype=labels.dtype,\\n                    device=labels.device,\\n                )\\n                multimodal_labels = torch.cat([labels[:, :1], projected_patch_labels, labels[:, 1:]], dim=1)\\n\\n            # Dispatch to Language Model\\n            language_model_output = self.language_model(\\n                input_ids=None,\\n                attention_mask=multimodal_attention_mask,\\n                position_ids=None,\\n                past_key_values=None,\\n                inputs_embeds=multimodal_embeddings,\\n                labels=multimodal_labels,\\n                use_cache=use_cache,\\n                output_attentions=output_attentions,\\n                output_hidden_states=output_hidden_states,\\n                return_dict=return_dict,\\n            )\\n\\n        # === Otherwise =>> Assume Invalid! ===\\n        elif (input_ids.shape[0] != pixel_values.shape[0]) or (inputs_embeds.shape[0] != pixel_values.shape[0]):\\n            raise ValueError(\\\"Non-homogenous batch of (text, image) input -- forward() does not support mixed batches!\\\")\\n\\n        else:\\n            raise ValueError(\\n                \\\"Invalid PrismaticForConditionalGeneration `forward()` call with provided arguments:\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `input_ids` = {input_ids is not None}\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `attention_mask` = {attention_mask is not None}\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `pixel_values` = {pixel_values is not None}\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `labels` = {labels is not None}\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `input_embeds` = {inputs_embeds is not None}\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `past_key_values` = {past_key_values is not None}\\\\n\\\"\\n                f\\\"=> `use_cache` = {use_cache}\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # Unpack `language_model_output` and return PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast (or tuple if not `return_dict`)\\n        if not return_dict:\\n            if output_projector_features and (projected_patch_embeddings is not None):\\n                return *language_model_output, projected_patch_embeddings\\n\\n            return language_model_output\\n\\n        return PrismaticCausalLMOutputWithPast(\\n            loss=language_model_output.loss,\\n            logits=language_model_output.logits,\\n            past_key_values=language_model_output.past_key_values,\\n            hidden_states=language_model_output.hidden_states,\\n            attentions=language_model_output.attentions,\\n            projector_features=projected_patch_embeddings,\\n        )\\n\\n    # === GenerationMixin Methods ===\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.61}}"}

```text
Now I understand the full picture!

The `PrismaticForConditionalGeneration.forward()` has three branches:
1. **Cached Generation** (`input_ids.shape[1] == 1` + `past_key_values is not None`): calls `self.language_model(input_ids=input_ids, ...)` - standard LLM generation step
2. **Unimodal Forward** (`pixel_values is None`): calls `self.language_model(input_ids=input_ids, ...)`


3. **Multimodal Forward** (`pixel_values is not None`): processes vision, builds multimodal embeddings, calls `self.language_model(inputs_embeds=multimodal_embeddings, ...)`

During `generate()`, the first call does a multimodal prefill with full input to build the KV cache, then subsequent calls use cached generation with single tokens. But my hook is showing all 7 calls with `hidden_states shape = [1, 1, 4096]`, which means the prefill call wasn't captured—it must be happening through the multimodal forward branch that calls `self.language_model()` with embeddings rather than input IDs.

I'm wondering if the `generate()` method in newer transformers handles the prefill differently than I expected, or if the first call is actually the multimodal forward but I'm miscounting because the shape stays `[1, 1]` throughout. With 7 action tokens generated, I'd expect 8 total calls if there's a prefill, so either it's being handled separately or the counting is off.

The real issue might be that my hook on `layer[1].self_attn` isn't catching the prefill call at all—maybe `LlamaModel` uses some optimization like `torch.compile` that bypasses the normal module call mechanism, or `generate()` uses a different code path like `_prefill()` that I'm not accounting for. I should add a hook on all layers to see if the prefill even goes through the model.

types\nlayer1_attn.forward = types.MethodType(ablated_forward, layer1_attn)\n\nimage = Image.new('RGB', (224,224), (128,128,128))\nprompt = 'In: What action should the robot take to open the drawer?\\nOut:'\ninputs = processor(prompt, image).to('cuda:0', dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n# Baseline (ablation inactive)\nablation_active[0] = False\nablation_calls[0] = 0\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    a1 = model.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key='bridge_orig', do_sample=False)\nprint(f'Baseline: {a1}, calls={ablation_calls[0]}')\n\n# Ablated\nablation_active[0] = True\nablation_calls[0] = 0\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    a2 = model.predict_action(**inputs, unnorm_key='bridge_orig', do_sample=False)\nprint(f'Ablated: {a2}, calls={ablation_calls[0]}')\nprint(f'Actions differ: {not np.allclose(a1, a2)}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "warn": true
}

- But monkey-patched forward methods do NOT get called


- The real culprit is that `prepare_inputs_for_generation` truncates input_ids to just the last token on every call because `DynamicCache` gets passed in from the start, not just after the first iteration

So the solution is two-pronged: first, fix `prepare_inputs_for_generation` to check the cache length instead of just whether it exists, so the full prefill happens on the first call when the ablation check can work. Second, use `register_forward_hook` on the attention module since that's confirmed to actually fire, rather than trying to monkey-patch the forward method directly. I'm realizing the forward hook might only be capturing the generation phase, not the prefill. If the model generates 7 tokens and each takes one forward pass, that accounts for the 7 calls showing `[1, 1, 4096]` shape, but the initial prompt processing must happen separately—possibly through compiled code that the hook doesn't catch. The issue is that OpenVLA's `prepare_inputs_for_generation` uses an outdated check—it just looks for whether `past_key_values` exists, not whether it actually contains cached data. I need to update that check to properly detect when the cache is empty versus populated, so the prefill processes the full sequence instead of truncating it to a single token. Once that's fixed, the ablation hook should work correctly since the attention mechanism will see the full sequence length during the initial forward pass.

Direct function replacement and instance method patching both fail to trigger, which suggests the transformers library is using a different dispatch mechanism that bypasses those interception points. Forward hooks are the only approach that's actually being called.

Given this constraint, I should use forward hooks for the ablation, but I'm also realizing there's a deeper issue with `prepare_inputs_for_generation` causing all calls to have seq_len=1. I could fix that first to restore proper prefill behavior, or I could work around it by leveraging the fact that even during cached generation, the attention weights still have access to the full KV cache length, so I can zero out attention to instruction positions in that dimension.

The real insight is that ablating during every forward pass—not just generation—would actually be more thorough, since it would affect the representations built up in the KV cache from the start, rather than only affecting the final generation tokens.

self.config.pad_to_multiple_of\\n\\n    def predict_action(\\n        self, input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None, **kwargs: str\\n    ) -> np.ndarray:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Thin wrapper around .generate() that decodes predicted actions and unnormalizes them.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # If the special empty token ('') does not already appear after the colon (':') token in the prompt\\n        # (after \\\"OUT:\\\" or \\\"ASSISTANT:\\\"), insert it to match the inputs seen at training time\\n        if not torch.all(input_ids[:, -1] == 29871):\\n            input_ids = torch.cat(\\n                (input_ids, torch.unsqueeze(torch.Tensor([29871]).long(), dim=0).to(input_ids.device)), dim=1\\n            )\\n\\n        # Run VLA inference\\n        generated_ids = self.generate(input_ids, max_new_tokens=self.get_action_dim(unnorm_key), **kwargs)\\n\\n        # Extract predicted action tokens and translate into (normalized) continuous actions\\n        predicted_action_token_ids = generated_ids[0, -self.get_action_dim(unnorm_key) :].cpu().numpy()\\n        discretized_actions = self.vocab_size - predicted_action_token_ids\\n        discretized_actions = np.clip(discretized_actions - 1, a_min=0, a_max=self.bin_centers.shape[0] - 1)\\n        normalized_actions = self.bin_centers[discretized_actions]\\n\\n        # Unnormalize actions\\n        action_norm_stats = self.get_action_stats(unnorm_key)\\n        mask = action_norm_stats.get(\\\"mask\\\", np.ones_like(action_norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"], dtype=bool))\\n        action_high, action_low = np.array(action_norm_stats[\\\"q99\\\"]), np.array(action_norm_stats[\\\"q01\\\"])\\n        actions = np.where(\\n            mask,\\n            0.5 * (normalized_actions + 1) * (action_high - action_low) + action_low,\\n            normalized_actions,\\n        )\\n\\n        return actions\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _check_unnorm_key(norm_stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], unnorm_key: Optional[str]) -> str:\\n        if unnorm_key is None:\\n            assert len(norm_stats) == 1, (\\n                f\\\"Your model was trained on more than one dataset, \\\"\\n                f\\\"please pass a `unnorm_key` from the following options to choose the statistics \\\"\\n                f\\\"used for un-normalizing actions: {norm_stats.keys()}\\\"\\n            )\\n            unnorm_key = next(iter(norm_stats.keys()))\\n\\n        assert unnorm_key in norm_stats, (\\n            f\\\"The `unnorm_key` you chose is not in the set of available dataset statistics, \\\"\\n            f\\\"please choose from: {norm_stats.keys()}\\\"\\n        )\\n        return unnorm_key\\n\\n    def get_action_dim(self, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Get the dimensionality of the policy's action space.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unnorm_key = self._check_unnorm_key(self.norm_stats, unnorm_key)\\n        return len(self.norm_stats[unnorm_key][\\\"action\\\"][\\\"q01\\\"])\\n\\n    def get_action_stats(self, unnorm_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Get all the logged statistics for the given dataset.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        unnorm_key = self._check_unnorm_key(self.norm_stats, unnorm_key)\\n        return self.norm_stats[unnorm_key][\\\"action\\\"]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.38}}"}

1. `prepare_inputs_for_generation` checks `if past_key_values is not None` → truncates input_ids to last token


2. In transformers 4.57.6, `generate()` always creates a DynamicCache upfront, so `past_key_values` is never None
3. This means even the first (prefill) forward call only sees 1 token → KV cache never gets populated → model generates garbage

But wait, the summary says actions ARE generated correctly (both baseline and ablated produce the same reasonable-looking actions). So the model IS working somehow. Let me re-examine the `prepare_inputs_for_generation` logic more carefully to understand how this is actually functioning.

If `past_key_values` is always not None due to the DynamicCache, then on the first call the input gets truncated to just the last token, the model processes only 1 token, and no KV cache entries are created for the other tokens. Yet the model still works, which suggests transformers 4.57.6's `generate()` must handle the prefill phase differently—perhaps with a separate initial call that bypasses the normal truncation logic, or the newer architecture processes the full prompt first before entering the generation loop. I see the issue now—OpenVLA's `prepare_inputs_for_generation` uses an outdated check that doesn't account for an empty cache, so it truncates to a single token on the first call even though the cache hasn't been populated yet. This causes the model to process only one token initially, then on subsequent calls it processes the accumulated input plus the newly generated token, but the logic breaks down because newer transformers expect the cache length check to distinguish between a non-existent cache and an empty one. Looking at the server logs showing all 7 forward calls with sequence length 1, it seems the prefill never actually processes the full prompt—the model only sees one token at a time. Yet it's producing consistent, valid actions for both baseline and ablated versions, which suggests the model might be ignoring context entirely and defaulting to the same action regardless of input. If that's what's happening, the fix becomes straightforward.

I need to check the `prepare_inputs_for_generation` implementation more carefully to see how it's handling the empty cache case, since that's likely where the prefill is getting skipped.
```

````text
Now I can see the exact issue. The Multimodal branch (around line 363) has:

```python
# === Handle Multimodal Forward ===
elif (input_ids.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0]) or (inputs_embeds.shape[0] == pixel_values.shape[0]):
    assert past_key_values is None, "Unexpected key `past_key_values` provided during language-only forward!"
```

The assertion message says "language-only forward" but it's in the MULTIMODAL branch. The assertion checks `past_key_values is None` — this was fine with the old transformers where past_key_values was None on the first call, but now DynamicCache (empty) is passed.


The fix is to update the assertion to accept an empty DynamicCache in addition to None, and correct the error message to reflect the multimodal context. When dispatching to the language model in this branch, passing `past_key_values=None` should work fine since the LLaMA model will create its own cache, but I need to verify how the `generate()` method handles this.

Actually, thinking through the flow: when `use_cache=True`, the language model will create and return a new cache with the processed tokens. On the next generation call, `prepare_inputs_for_generation` receives this populated cache, truncates input_ids to just 1 token, and the forward pass goes to the cached generation branch instead. So the real fix is ensuring the assertion allows empty caches and that the multimodal branch properly initializes the cache for subsequent calls. the language model creates its own DynamicCache, populates it, and returns it—which means generate() discards the original cache it created and uses this new one instead. The real question is whether generate() expects the cache object to be modified in-place or if it can accept a different cache object in the output. Looking at transformers 4.57.6, the DynamicCache.update() method modifies in-place, so generate() might be holding a reference to its original cache and expecting that specific object to be populated. The safest approach would be to pass the DynamicCache through to the language model rather than None, ensuring the original cache object gets updated directly. The language model will receive the empty DynamicCache and populate it as it processes each layer, so generate() can then use that populated cache for subsequent calls—this should resolve the disconnect between what generate() expects and what the model creates.

torch.argmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n        generated_ids.append(next_token.squeeze().item())\n        attn_mask = torch.ones((1, past_key_values[0][0].shape[2] + 1),\n                               device=input_ids.device, dtype=input_ids.dtype)\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(\n                input_ids=next_token,\n                attention_mask=attn_mask,\n                pixel_values=None,\n                past_key_values=past_key_values,\n                use_cache=True,\n            )\n        past_key_values = outputs.past_key_values\n        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[:, -1, :]\n\n    predicted_action_token_ids = np.array(generated_ids)\n    discretized_actions = model.vocab_size - predicted_action_token_ids\n    discretized_actions = np.clip(discretized_actions - 1, a_min=0, a_max=model.bin_centers.shape[0] - 1)\n    normalized_actions = model.bin_centers[discretized_actions]\n    action_norm_stats = model.get_action_stats(unnorm_key)\n    mask = action_norm_stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(action_norm_stats[\"q01\"], dtype=bool))\n    action_high, action_low = np.array(action_norm_stats[\"q99\"]), np.array(action_norm_stats[\"q01\"])\n    actions = np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized_actions + 1) * (action_high - action_low) + action_low, normalized_actions)\n    return actions\n\n# Test with a dummy image\nimg = Image.new(\"RGB\", (224, 224), color=(128, 128, 128))\ninstruction = \"pick up the red cup and place it on the shelf\"\nprompt = f\"In: What action should the robot take to {instruction}?\\nOut:\"\ninputs = processor(prompt, img).to(\"cuda:0\", dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n# === Test 1: Baseline ===\naction_baseline = predict_action_manual(model, inputs[\"input_ids\"], inputs[\"pixel_values\"], UNNORM_KEY)\nprint(f\"Baseline: {action_baseline}\")\n\n# === Test 2: Ablated ===\nimport transformers.models.llama.modeling_llama as llama_mod\nfrom transformers.models.llama.modeling_llama import repeat_kv\noriginal_fn = llama_mod.eager_attention_forward\n\nlayer1_attn = model.language_model.model.layers[1].self_attn\nlayer1_attn._ablate_heads = INSTRUCTION_HEADS_24\n\nablation_count = 0\ndef ablated_eager(module, query, key, value, attention_mask, scaling, dropout=0.0, **kwargs):\n    global ablation_count\n    key_states = repeat_kv(key, module.num_key_value_groups)\n    value_states = repeat_kv(value, module.num_key_value_groups)\n    attn_weights = torch.matmul(query, key_states.transpose(2, 3)) * scaling\n    if attention_mask is not None:\n        causal_mask = attention_mask[:, :, :, :key_states.shape[-2]]\n        attn_weights = attn_weights + causal_mask\n    attn_weights = nn.functional.softmax(attn_weights, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(query.dtype)\n    attn_weights = nn.functional.dropout(attn_weights, p=dropout, training=module.training)\n    if hasattr(module, '_ablate_heads') and module._ablate_heads is not None:\n        kv_len = attn_weights.shape[-1]\n        if INST_START < kv_len:\n            ablation_count += 1\n            attn_weights[:, module._ablate_heads, :, INST_START:] = 0.0\n            row_sums = attn_weights.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-9)\n            attn_weights = attn_weights / row_sums\n    attn_output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, value_states)\n    attn_output = attn_output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()\n    return attn_output, attn_weights\n\nllama_mod.eager_attention_forward = ablated_eager\n\naction_ablated = predict_action_manual(model, inputs[\"input_ids\"], inputs[\"pixel_values\"], UNNORM_KEY)\nprint(f\"Ablated:  {action_ablated}\")\nprint(f\"Ablation triggers: {ablation_count}\")\nprint(f\"Actions differ: {not np.allclose(action_baseline, action_ablated)}\")\nprint(f\"Max diff: {np.max(np.abs(action_baseline - action_ablated)):.6f}\")\n\n# Restore\nllama_mod.eager_attention_forward = original_fn\nlayer1_attn._ablate_heads = None\nPYEOF",

```text
The compat patch for robosuite has changed. In the previous session, `load_controller_config` was removed and `load_part_controller_config` was the new name. \"/root/autodl-tmp/LIBERO\")\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HDF5_USE_FILE_LOCKING\"] = \"FALSE\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b\"\nUNNORM_KEY = \"bridge_orig\"\nNUM_IMAGE_TOKENS = 256\nINST_START = 1 + NUM_IMAGE_TOKENS\nINSTRUCTION_HEADS_24 = [0,1,2,4,7,8,10,11,12,14,15,16,17,18,19,20,22,23,25,26,27,29,30,31]\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\nimport libero.libero\nLIB_DIR = os.path.dirname(libero.libero.__file__)\n\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n\nprint(\"Loading model...\")\nprocessor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, trust_remote_code=True)\nmodel = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, trust_remote_code=True, low_cpu_mem_usage=True\n).to(\"cuda:0\").eval()\n\ndef predict_action_manual(model, input_ids, pixel_values, unnorm_key):\n    action_dim = model.get_action_dim(unnorm_key)\n    if not torch.all(input_ids[:, -1] == 29871):\n        input_ids = torch.cat(\n            (input_ids, torch.tensor([[29871]], device=input_ids.device, dtype=input_ids.dtype)), dim=1)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=torch.ones_like(input_ids),\n                        pixel_values=pixel_values, past_key_values=None, use_cache=True)\n    past_key_values = outputs.past_key_values\n    next_token_logits = outputs.logits[:, -1, :]\n    generated_ids = []\n    for _ in range(action_dim):\n        next_token = torch.argmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n        generated_ids.append(next_token.squeeze().item())\n        attn_mask = torch.ones((1, past_key_values[0][0].shape[2] + 1),\n                               device=input_ids.device, dtype=input_ids.dtype)\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            outputs = model(input_ids=next_token, attention_mask=attn_mask,\n                            pixel_values=None, past_key_values=past_key_values, use_cache=True)\n        past_key_values = outputs.past_key_values\n        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[:, -1, :]\n    predicted_action_token_ids = np.array(generated_ids)\n    discretized_actions = model.vocab_size - predicted_action_token_ids\n    discretized_actions = np.clip(discretized_actions - 1, a_min=0, a_max=model.bin_centers.shape[0] - 1)\n    normalized_actions = model.bin_centers[discretized_actions]\n    action_norm_stats = model.get_action_stats(unnorm_key)\n    mask = action_norm_stats.get(\"mask\", np.ones_like(action_norm_stats[\"q01\"], dtype=bool))\n    action_high, action_low = np.array(action_norm_stats[\"q99\"]), np.array(action_norm_stats[\"q01\"])\n    return np.where(mask, 0.5 * (normalized_actions + 1) * (action_high - action_low) + action_low, normalized_actions)\n\n# Test with a real LIBERO task\nbench = benchmark.get_benchmark(\"libero_goal\")()\ntask = bench.get_task(0)\nbddl_path = os.path.join(LIB_DIR, \"bddl_files\", task.problem_folder, task.bddl_file)\nenv = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=bddl_path, camera_heights=224, camera_widths=224, camera_names=\"agentview\")\n\nobs = env.reset()\nimage = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\ninstruction = task.language\nprint(f\"Task: {task.name}, Instruction: {instruction}\")\n\n# Baseline step\nimg_pil = Image.fromarray(image)\nprompt = f\"In: What action should the robot take to {instruction}?\\nOut:\"\ninputs = processor(prompt, img_pil).to(\"cuda:0\", dtype=torch.bfloat16)\naction = predict_action_manual(model, inputs[\"input_ids\"], inputs[\"pixel_values\"], UNNORM_KEY)\nprint(f\"Baseline action: {action}\")\n\nobs2, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\nprint(f\"Env step OK: reward={reward}\")\n\n# Test 3 steps to verify loop works\nfor i in range(2):\n    image = obs2[\"agentview_image\"]\n    img_pil = Image.fromarray(image)\n    inputs = processor(prompt, img_pil).to(\"cuda:0\", dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    action = predict_action_manual(model, inputs[\"input_ids\"], inputs[\"pixel_values\"], UNNORM_KEY)\n    obs2, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\n    print(f\"Step {i+2}: action={action[:3]}..., reward={reward}\")\n\nenv.close()\nprint(\"Full pipeline test PASSED\")\nPYEOF",
  "timeout": 120000
}
